# module-kit's decision log

> Why module-kit is built the way it is. Each entry says what was decided, why, what was rejected and where it is described.
>
> **Rule:** do not re-propose what was rejected without new facts. If a decision has to change, ask the author and update this file. An entry is never rewritten: an amendment with its date at the end of the entry corrects it, or a new entry replaces it, and the old one stays where it is, marked "Superseded".
>
> The repository was set up 2026-10-19 from the template of `spec` v0.1.0 (R21 spec), moved to `spec` v0.2.6 on 2026-10-20 (R01), and the same day to v0.2.9 (R02) and to v0.2.11 (R03). On 2026-10-21 its first code came — the skeleton of the CLI (R04) —, and the same day it moved to v0.2.13 (R05), and CodeQL began to read Go (R06); since then its releases are put out by the shared `release.yml` of `.github` (R07). On 2026-11-02 it moved to `spec` v0.6.3, the line 0.6 with module format 0.2 (R08), and the same day decided what to write the package's canonical text with (R09) and what to execute the random generator with (R10), and then the clock, the slots and the version of the Starlark sandbox (R11). The same day it moved to v0.6.5 (R12) and decided how to compare module versions by semver (R13), and then moved to v0.6.6 (R14) and summed up the result of the detailing — the command of open views (R15) and the code epic (R16). On 2026-11-04 it moved to `spec` v0.9.2, the line 0.9 with module format 0.3, and with that began the code epic (R17), and the same day prepared CI for the code: the coverage of the tests went to Codecov, the release began to be built by the list `paths` (R18), and gopls went into the image from its own lock file (R19). At the same time the code took the format and the vectors as the Go modules of `spec`, and the library got its Go packages and the vector test with the skip list (R20), and then the YAML reader on go-yaml v4 with passes of its own around it (R21) and the module check by its layout and the window of format versions (R22), and then the check of the parts' files by the format's schemas, with a copy of the schemas in a package of its own (R23), and the open view of the parts by the marks `x-open` in the same schemas (R24), and then the rules of the manifest, the skill graph and the catalogs, and the tiers of skills (R25), and the rules of a task and of a lesson (R26), and then the verdict of the kinds `number`, `fraction` and `choice`, with normalization and the version of Unicode that the release names, and the rules of a task that the slot check shows by itself (R27), and then the Starlark sandbox on go.starlark.net, pinned in `go.mod`, and the verdict of a check predicate in it (R28). On 2026-11-05 the validity of an observation, the join of two readings and the verdict by an observation came as a package of their own, `observation`, over `verdict` (R29), and the same day the leak check came as the package `leak`, with the rules `task.no-leaks` and `lesson.no-leaks` and normalization in an internal package of its own (R30), and the generator as code of its own in `internal/random`, with the instance number of an attempt in the package `template` (R31), and then task templates in the same package, with the expressions in the Starlark sandbox and the rules of a task on each instance (R32), and template solvers there too, on one thread of the sandbox per template (R33), and then the canonical text as a package of its own, the package build and reading a package by the chain of digests, and the digest of a task's open view (R34), and then the build and the reading of the module index and the version of a new attempt as the package `moduleindex`, with the parsing of module versions in `internal/semver` (R35), and then version comparison as the package `moduleversion`: the version part by the format's table, the report by places and the steps of 0.y.z (R36), and then the rules `module-version.*` and reading a package of a previous format version by the places of all versions, with which all the format's vectors are passed (R37), and then the CLI got the commands `check` and `build`, solvers one per CPU and the licenses of third-party modules in the release (R38), and then the warning about a picture with transparent pixels, as the package `picture` (R39), and then the commands `index` and `diff` (R40), and then running each binary on its own OS in CI (R41). On 2026-11-06 the vector test stopped skipping cases, and the releases moved to the line 0.2: its first release, v0.2.0, is the first that others take (R42). The same day the CLI got the command `open` — the open views of a release of `modules` for the samples page of `canvas` (R43), the devcontainer image began to be built in CI on arm64 too (R44), go-yaml moved to v4.0.0 (R45), and an anchor with a colon at the end of its name began to name a rule of its own (R46). The same day it moved to `spec` v0.9.3, which answered all its questions В19–В34 (R47), and on 2026-11-08 to v0.9.8 (R48) and the same day to v0.9.9, which says to warn about a number in a text of the whole module (R49), and then this warning went into the report of the module check (R50). On 2026-11-09 it moved to v0.9.16, the last tag of the line 0.9, with the answers of the epic E7 of `spec` to its questions, and gopls in the devcontainer became the template's (R51), and the same day to v0.12.0 with module format 0.4, and the releases to the line 0.3 (R52). On 2026-11-11 it moved to v0.15.0, where the format is the same (R53), and on 2026-11-12 to v0.16.3, the line 0.16, with Go 1.27.4; the way of work and the structure of the documents that the line gives the repositories are taken by a task of their own (R54). The same day it took the way of work and the structure of the documents of the line 0.16 — `.claude/settings.json`, the rules of parts, RUN.md and `DEBT.md` by the template, sources and measurements in files of their own, the budgets of size —, and left the translation of `docs/` to the task T44 (R55). How it comes to differ from the template over time — by tools in the devcontainer, by rules in RUN.md — is recorded here as a decision, as R46 canvas-api recorded the differences from its pattern. Next number — R56.
>
> Here entries are cited as `R03`, in other repositories as `R03 module-kit`. The decisions of `spec` are cited as "R18 spec", MathTrail's decisions as "R37 canvas-api", and in a list as "canvas-api: R37, R38". Each log has its own numbers.
>
> The open questions that concern several repositories are in the registers of `spec`: О- in product 13 spec, П- in privacy 9 spec, Т- in repositories 8 spec. What is still to decide in the component itself is in [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 2.

## The tag of `spec`

**R01. `module-kit` is on `spec` v0.2.6: the submodule `spec/` is at this tag, the links to `spec` pinned to a tag name it, and what came from the template is as in the template of v0.2.6. Since then the documents are checked by `just -f spec/justfile docs-check-repo`.**
- **What the tag brings `module-kit`** (`git diff v0.1.0 v0.2.6` in `spec/`, read 2026-10-20; Д6 in table 4 of RUN.md of `spec`):
  - R32 spec — a repository's documents are checked by the `docs-check` of the `spec` its submodule is on: the links, its own sections and log, the tables «В» and «Д», and the citations of `spec` against the submodule at its tag. An example of a citation is written in inline code, so that the check does not take it for a citation (RULES.md, Citations);
  - R30 spec — the CI checks are the same for all repositories, and `module-kit` will have them even before code: the secret search, DCO, CodeQL, Scorecard and the documents check are shared checks from `.github`. Scorecard is shared if its server accepts the result of a shared workflow, and otherwise it is a copy in the repository (architecture 3.11 spec). The merge is held by all the checks but Scorecard, the quality gate of SonarCloud included. T17 `spec` will connect them: its session writes the CI files here, through `.repos/module-kit`, together with `dependabot.yml` and the exclusion of `spec/**` for SonarCloud (Д7 in table 4 of RUN.md of `spec`), and it also sets the ruleset of the branch `main`. Until it, `module-kit` has no CI. The shared workflows for code — the Go wrapper over the repository's recipes, with the license check, and the release with a signature — come with the first code of `module-kit` and are tested on it (repositories 6 spec; architecture 3.11 spec);
  - R31 spec — a release of `spec` comes out by itself, after green CI on `main`: the next patch of the line, immutable, with GitHub's attestation. A repository still moves to a new tag by a task of its own;
  - R27 spec — the random generator and the derivation of seeds from `spec` are one for the whole platform: `module-kit`, the widgets, `engine` and `sim`. The algorithm is chosen by the epic E2 in RUN.md of `spec`, with the recommendations of two tasks — T02 of this repository and T03 engine —, and it takes into account the scheme of standalone's simulator, FNV-64a and PCG; the recommendation of Т-18 is extended with the same. This is an input of T02;
  - R25 spec — the samples page of `canvas` for the first measurement gets the tasks and the media files at build, from a release of `modules` through the CLI of `module-kit`, and makes no network calls while it works. CI of `canvas` is a new consumer in the card 3.7 spec. Whether the CLI needs a command of its own for open views, R25 spec left to the detailing of `module-kit`: this is [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 2.5, and T05 decides it;
  - the row of the epic E2 in table 2 of RUN.md of `spec` — the answers to В19 and В8 `spec`: T01 and T02 run at the same time as E2, not before it, and what names the digests of both parts of a package has a term of its own in concepts 15 spec, without the word "manifest", with the package's schema in the same epic (Т-15; architecture 3.10 spec);
  - Т-27 — what checks the signature of a package, which the shared release workflow of `.github` puts; the card 3.7 spec now refers to it. The recommendation is to check both the SAN — the path of the shared `release.yml` of `.github` at the SHA of one of the releases of `.github` — and the caller's repository and workflow; the condition is immutable releases of `.github` and a ruleset of its tags `v*`. To be decided with the first release of `module-kit`;
  - R28 spec — `learner-model` sets the graph's types, and `engine` takes them from it; they are still simple and not from `module-kit` (architecture 3.8 spec);
  - RULES.md — an example of a citation is written in code (Citations). A development or CI tool that never ships and puts none of its own code into what ships may also carry another OSI-approved license, or free terms for open source, but only as a named exception: in the license check, or in the decision that brings it in (Hard rules; R12 spec). This concerns the tools of the future code of `module-kit`;
  - R23, R24, R26 and R29 spec — about other components: the success corridor, the key for eval, an account with the owner profile and the children's profiles, the reason codes. They do not change the documents of `module-kit`;
  - devcontainer — the base image `devcontainers/base` 3.0.10 by its digest; Claude Code is installed without the packages' scripts, its `install.cjs` is run by name, and `claude --version` stops the build on the stub; `git.alwaysSignOff` — a commit from Source Control is signed off by DCO (R22 spec, R30 spec).
- **What changed here:**
  - `spec/` — at v0.2.6, commit 4e55421. The links to `spec` in README.md and the tag in CLAUDE.md and RUN.md — v0.2.6. That the repository was set up from the template of v0.1.0, the log's opening still says: this is history;
  - five examples of citations of the old template — in inline code: two in CLAUDE.md, Documents, two in «Ссылках» of RUN.md and one in the log's opening. Rule 6 of RUN.md and the section Environment of CLAUDE.md name `docs-check-repo`. These places are as in the template of v0.2.6;
  - `.devcontainer/` — the one that the recipe of `spec` v0.2.6 (`editDevcontainer`) builds for `module-kit` of the kind `go`. The name of the container and of the network, `postStartCommand` and the Go extension are its own, as on v0.1.0 too. The move introduces no differences from the template;
  - what the tag decided for `module-kit` is named in its documents: T02 — R27 spec; T05 — the command of open views (R25 spec), the change of the card for the points beyond it, and Т-27 for the next epic; T01 — the term without the word "manifest" and CI of `canvas` among those that read the package; «Почему такой порядок» and «Не входит» in RUN.md; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 1, 2, 2.1 and 2.2 and the new section 2.5.

— *Why now:* T17 `spec` connects the shared checks from `.github` v0.1.0 here (R30 spec). The shared `documents.yml` builds the stage `toolchain` from the `.devcontainer/Dockerfile` of whoever called it and runs `just -f spec/justfile docs-check-repo` in it ([`documents.yml`](https://github.com/altnovatrail/.github/blob/ff2a359/.github/workflows/documents.yml), the release v0.1.0 of `.github`, commit ff2a359, read 2026-10-20). And this recipe exists in `spec` only since v0.2.3 (R32 spec): on v0.1.0 the required check would be red and would hold any merge. v0.2.6 is the latest release of `spec` on 2026-10-20, and it is immutable (`gh release view v0.2.6 -R altnovatrail/spec`); `gh release verify v0.2.6 -R altnovatrail/spec` confirmed it by its attestation, and the tag is on commit 4e55421. The latest release at the time of the task, and none earlier than the one that has the answers to В8 and В19 `spec`, is also what Д6 in table 4 of RUN.md of `spec` says to take.

— *Rejected:*
- to stay on v0.1.0 until the first code: the documents check from T17 `spec` would hold any merge, T01 and T02 would read `spec` without R27 spec and without the answer about the term, and a link to a section of `spec` that does not exist at the tag, or a gap in the log, would pass unnoticed (Д6 in table 4 of RUN.md of `spec`);
- v0.2.3 and v0.2.4: they have `docs-check-repo`, but not the answers to В8 and В19 `spec`. v0.2.5: the answers are there, but Т-27, which the card 3.7 spec now refers to, is not, and Д6 says to take the latest release;
- to move without the devcontainer, or to carry over its changes selectively: the container of `module-kit` is the container of `spec` (CLAUDE.md, Environment), and a difference would have to be recorded as a decision, and there is no reason for one;
- examples of citations in guillemets, as before: `docs-check-repo` checks a number in guillemets against the log, and R32 spec rejected not checking them.

— *Consequence:* T06 goes first in table 1 of RUN.md, and T01 depends on it. How T03–T05 stand now, the author decides — В1 in table 3 of RUN.md. The devcontainer image was not built here — Д1 in table 4 of RUN.md, until Rebuild Container. The comment about Claude Code in its Dockerfile is inexact, but the Dockerfile is a copy of `spec`'s, and the fix goes there — В2 in table 3 of RUN.md, «в реестр spec». The CI files — the calls of the shared workflows, `dependabot.yml`, `.sonarcloud.properties` — are written here by T17 `spec`, not by a task of this repository. — RUN.md, T06, T01, T02, T05; CLAUDE.md; README.md; `.devcontainer/`; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 1, 2, 2.1, 2.2 and 2.5.

The condition for Scorecard is met: its server accepts the result of a shared workflow called from another repository, and `module-kit` calls the shared `scorecard.yml` of `.github` and keeps no copy (architecture 3.11 spec at v0.2.11).

— *Amendment 2026-11-02:* `module-kit` is on `spec` v0.6.3 (R08), and what is said above about v0.2.13 is history too. Т-27, which R01 called open, is closed by R77 spec: the signature of a release file is checked by the recipe `release-verify` of `spec`, and the library `module-kit` checks no signatures. **R02. `module-kit` is on `spec` v0.2.9: the submodule `spec/` is at this tag, the links to `spec` pinned to a tag name it, and what came from the template is as in the template of v0.2.9. With it RUN.md has rule 12: before a new task, close the findings of SonarCloud, CodeQL and Scorecard on the previous one.**
- **What the tag brings `module-kit`** (`git diff v0.2.6 v0.2.9` in `spec/`, read 2026-10-20; Д6 in table 4 of RUN.md of `spec`):
  - rule 12 of the template's RUN.md, since v0.2.7: before a new task, close the findings of SonarCloud, CodeQL and Scorecard on the previous one. A finding is first fixed by a change, and one that a change cannot close is left open and its reason is recorded: a reason shared by all repositories in R33 spec, one of its own in this log, as R51 canvas-api. The author commits the fixes of findings apart from the task;
  - R33 spec, since v0.2.8, — the analysers' findings that stay open in all repositories with the shared checks, and why. The list was also checked against the first run of `module-kit` on `main`: commit 254452e, Scorecard score 6.3. The seven findings open here on 2026-10-20 are all Scorecard's and all from this list: Code-Review and Branch-Protection, Pinned-Dependencies — about `npm install -g` of Claude Code in `.devcontainer/Dockerfile`, Maintained, CI-Tests, CII-Best-Practices and Fuzzing (`gh api 'repos/altnovatrail/module-kit/code-scanning/alerts?state=open'`). SonarCloud has no open findings. The reason for Fuzzing in R33 spec — there is no code yet that parses others' input — holds for `module-kit` while it has no code at all: its very first code reads authors' modules, and fuzz tests will come with it;
  - the 2026-10-20 amendment to R30 spec — what holds the merge. The shared checks and "SonarCloud Code Analysis" are required, the findings of CodeQL hold the merge by a rule of the ruleset — errors and vulnerabilities of high and above —, and merges are made only by a merge commit: DCO checks the commits of a pull request, and a squash would put on `main` a commit whose sign-off nobody checked. The ruleset `main` here is 25488826, set by T17 `spec`: "Secrets / gitleaks", "DCO / sign-off", "Documents / docs-check-repo", "CodeQL / analyze (actions)" and "SonarCloud Code Analysis" are required (GitHub's API, 2026-10-20);
  - T17 `spec` is done: the shared checks from `.github` v0.1.0 are connected here by pull request #2, commit 254452e — the calling files, `dependabot.yml` and `.sonarcloud.properties` with `spec/**` (Д7 in table 4 of RUN.md of `spec`). [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 1, «Не входит» of the epic E1 and «Почему такой порядок» in RUN.md spoke of it in the future tense. The nine other repositories are connected by T21 `spec`, each after its own move to the tag; `module-kit` is not part of it;
  - Д9 in table 4 of RUN.md of `spec` — CodeQL does not read Go yet: the shared `codeql.yml` v0.1.0 takes, beyond `actions`, only `javascript-typescript`. When it accepts Go, `module-kit` will add `go` to the input `languages`, and the ruleset `main` "CodeQL / analyze (go)" (R30 spec); until then the first Go code will come to `main` without CodeQL's analysis. Here this is the debt Д2 in table 4 of RUN.md;
  - Д10 there too — the sign-off of a Dependabot commit under the check "DCO / sign-off" and its raising of the link to `.github` have not been tried in practice. The first pull request of Dependabot in a repository with the shared checks will show it, `module-kit` among them;
  - the 2026-10-20 amendment to R31 spec — about the releases of `spec` and `.github`: the `release.yml` of `spec` no longer checks out the event's commit, and the recipe `release-version` counts the tags of the repository it is called from and, outside `spec`, requires `RELEASE_LINE`. It does not change the documents of `module-kit`: the release will come to it with the first code, through the shared workflow of `.github` (architecture 3.11 spec);
  - the tag decides no tasks of `module-kit` and closes no rows of table 3 of RUN.md. The template changed only by rule 12, and the `.devcontainer/` of `spec` and the recipe `editDevcontainer` did not change.
- **What changed here:**
  - `spec/` — at v0.2.9, commit 5eafb1a. The tag in README.md, CLAUDE.md, at the start of RUN.md and in the status of [architecture.md](architecture.md) — v0.2.9;
  - rule 12 — in RUN.md, as in the template of v0.2.9, with `module-kit` in the links to SonarCloud and to the findings: it came by a three-way merge of the template v0.2.6 → v0.2.9 with the files of `module-kit`;
  - [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 1, «Не входит» of the epic E1 and «Почему такой порядок» in RUN.md: the shared checks were connected by T17 `spec`. Section 1 also names rule 12 and R33 spec for the findings, and Go in CodeQL — Д2;
  - tables 3 and 4 of RUN.md: В1 — that T21 is left in table 1 of RUN.md of `spec` at v0.2.9, В2 — that the comment is the same at v0.2.9 too; Д1 is closed — the container is rebuilt —, and the new Д2 is Go in CodeQL, by Д9 `spec`;
  - `.devcontainer/` did not change: it matches byte for byte what `editDevcontainer` v0.2.9 builds for `module-kit` of the kind `go`.

— *Why now:* `module-kit` has neither rule 12 nor R33 spec: they came out in `spec` after v0.2.6, and T16 `spec` recorded that they will come to `module-kit` and `tutor` with the next move to a tag (Д6 in table 4 of RUN.md of `spec`). Without rule 12 the next task will start without sorting out the findings of the previous one, and a finding whose reason is not in R33 spec cannot be told from an answered one. The author assigned the move to the latest release of `spec` on 2026-10-20. v0.2.9 is the latest on that date, and it is immutable (`gh release view -R altnovatrail/spec`); `gh release verify v0.2.9 -R altnovatrail/spec` confirmed it by its attestation, and the tag is on commit 5eafb1a.

— *Rejected:*
- to stay on v0.2.6 until the first code: nobody would close the analysers' findings on the previous task, and R33 spec could not be cited — it does not exist at v0.2.6, and `docs-check-repo` would not let such a citation pass;
- v0.2.7 or v0.2.8: v0.2.7 has no R33 spec, and rule 12 of the template does not refer to it yet; v0.2.8 has no T21 `spec` and no amendment to R30 spec about it, and Д6 says to take the latest release;
- to write rule 12 in our own words: it is the template's text, and a difference from it would have to be recorded as a decision, as R46 canvas-api, and there is no reason for one.

— *Consequence:* rule 12 holds from the next task: it starts with the findings on this one. В1 and В2 stay in table 3 of RUN.md: the tag did not decide them, and the Dockerfile of `spec` v0.2.9 carries the same comment about Claude Code. Д1 in table 4 of RUN.md is closed, and Д2 is open until the shared `codeql.yml` accepts Go. — RUN.md, T07, rule 12, tables 3 and 4; CLAUDE.md; README.md; [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 1.

**R03. `module-kit` is on `spec` v0.2.11: the submodule `spec/` is at this tag, the links to `spec` pinned to a tag name it, and `.devcontainer/` is the one that the recipe of `spec` v0.2.11 builds for `module-kit`. In its Dockerfile each archive is checked against a sha256 checksum for each architecture, and the fixed comment on `install.cjs` closes В2 in table 3 of RUN.md.**
- **What the tag brings `module-kit`** (`git diff v0.2.9 v0.2.11` in `spec/`, read 2026-10-20; Д11 and Д12 in table 4 of RUN.md of `spec`):
  - R34 spec, since v0.2.11, — an archive that `.devcontainer/Dockerfile` downloads is checked before unpacking against a sha256 checksum from the publisher's own list: an `ARG` beside the version, for each architecture, `amd64` and `arm64`. A wrong checksum stops the build, and the checksum changes in the same change as the version, by hand: Dependabot does not see these versions. RULES.md names the checksum in the rule of exact versions (Hard rules). For `module-kit` these are Node and just in the stage `toolchain`, gh and just-lsp in the stage `devcontainer`. Claude Code is installed from the npm registry, as before, and the Scorecard finding about `npm install -g` stays open with its reason (R33 spec);
  - the answer to В2 is a fix of the `.devcontainer/Dockerfile` of `spec` itself (Д11 at the start of table 4 of RUN.md of `spec`). The comment now says that without `install.cjs` a stub stays in the place of `claude` — a shell script that reports that there is no native binary and exits with code 1 —, and a person runs the package's Node wrapper themselves, as a fallback. This is what В2 recommended;
  - architecture 3.11 spec and repositories 6 spec — the answers to В14 and В15 `.github`. The shared workflows for code come with the code they are tested on (R03 .github): the Go wrapper and the release with the first code of `module-kit`, the TypeScript wrapper with the first TypeScript code, in `canvas`. At v0.2.9, by repositories 6 spec, all the shared workflows for code came with the first code of `module-kit` — the Go and TypeScript wrappers and the release —, and [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 1, said so. Scorecard is shared: its server accepts the result of a shared workflow called from another repository (architecture 3.11 spec), and the condition that R01 named is met;

## Code and checks

**R04. The first code of `module-kit` is the skeleton of a CLI in Go, ahead of the module format: `cmd/module-kit`, which for now only answers `--version`. Go 1.27.3 is in the stage `toolchain` of the devcontainer, and the checks are recipes of its own, `ci-lint`, `ci-test`, `ci-vuln` and `ci-licenses`, which the shared `go.yml` of `.github` runs. This is the author's decision of 2026-10-21, and the task was done on the author's instruction by the session of `.github` — an exception to RULES.md, Across repositories.**
- **What was set up:**
  - the Go module `github.com/altnovatrail/module-kit`, without dependencies. `go.mod` has `go 1.27.0`: this is the lower bound for those who import the library, and Go 1.27.3 itself is pinned in the stage `toolchain`;
  - `cmd/module-kit` — the CLI. For now it only answers `module-kit --version` and prints `module-kit dev`: the release will write the release version into it when it builds it. On any other command it writes a hint to stderr and exits with code 2. The test is `main_test.go` beside it;
  - `justfile`:
    - `build` builds the CLI into `bin/`, and `.gitignore` keeps it out of git;
    - `ci-lint` — gofmt over the `.go` files that git tracks or would track, without `testdata/`: git names no files of the submodule `spec/` in this list. And `go vet`;
    - `ci-test` — `go test -race -count=1 ./...`;
    - `ci-vuln` — govulncheck v1.9.0: known vulnerabilities in the code that the module reaches;
    - `ci-licenses` — go-licenses v2.0.1: the license of each package the module is built from must be in the list `ALLOWED_LICENSES`, which the shared `go.yml` gives (R16 .github). Without the list the recipe stops and says where to get it.

    The tools run through `go run` at the module's exact version, as in the `justfile` of canvas-api ([architecture.md](architecture.md), section 4): nothing is installed into the image, the Go module proxy serves exactly this version, and Go's checksum database vouches for it;
  - `.devcontainer/`:
    - in the stage `toolchain` — Go 1.27.3. Its archive is checked against a sha256 checksum for each architecture from the list of go.dev/dl, as the other archives are (R34 spec), and `GOTOOLCHAIN=local` keeps the command `go` from downloading another toolchain;
    - gopls is not in the image. SonarCloud flags `go install …@v<version>` in a Dockerfile by the rule docker:S8545 as a dependency without a lock file, and its required check would hold the merge. The Go extension will offer to install gopls itself at the first `.go` file — Д5 in table 4 of RUN.md;
    - this is a difference of the container of `module-kit` from the container of `spec` (CLAUDE.md, Environment). On a move to a new tag of `spec`, the Dockerfile is taken from the recipe and these lines are put in again;
  - `.github/dependabot.yml` — the ecosystem `gomod` with a three-day pause, as the others have. Dependabot does not see Go in the Dockerfile and the tools in the `justfile`: they are raised by hand;
  - `.github/workflows/ci.yml` — the job "Go", which calls the shared `go.yml` of `.github`. The required checks "Go / lint", "Go / test", "Go / vuln" and "Go / licenses" are put in the ruleset `main` by the author (rule 4 of RUN.md; R30 spec).

— *Why:* the author's decision of 2026-10-21. The shared `go.yml` is written by T15 `.github`, and the author decided to test it now, on the skeleton of the CLI, rather than wait for the first code of the format, which will come with an epic after E1 (RUN.md, T10; R16 .github). The skeleton is not a throwaway hello world: the card names a CLI for authors in `module-kit` (architecture 3.7 spec), and this code stays. Go 1.27.3 is the newest stable release on 2026-10-21, and the versions of the tools are the newest on the same date ([architecture.md](architecture.md), section 4).

— *An exception to the rules.* RULES.md, Across repositories: "a session changes neither spec nor any other repository from where it works". The author gave this task to the session of `.github`. It ran in a temporary clone of `module-kit`, in the devcontainer of `.github`, whose Dockerfile before this task differed from the one here by one line of a comment. The branch, the commits, the push and the pull request were made by this session, and the merge and the ruleset by the author. The rules of this repository — CLAUDE.md, RUN.md and this log — it read and worked by. The next tasks of `module-kit` again run in its own session.

— *Rejected:*
- to wait for the first code of the format: so the plan stood (R03 .github; T15 .github), but this is an epic after E1, and the author decided to test the shared `go.yml` earlier;
- a throwaway hello world: it would be removed later, while the skeleton of the CLI stays;
- gopls in the image through `go install …@v<version>`. SonarCloud flags this by the rule docker:S8545, and "SonarCloud Code Analysis" holds the merge; with `-mod=readonly`, as in canvas-api, the finding is the same. gopls built inside a module of its own does not know its version and prints `(devel)`. Rule 12 of RUN.md leaves marking a finding as false as a last resort, and gopls can be pinned otherwise too — Д5;
- `go 1.27.3` in `go.mod`: each patch of Go here would become a lower bound for `api` and the eval runner of `tutor`, which import the library;
- golangci-lint in `ci-lint`: gofmt and go vet are enough for the skeleton, and golangci-lint is under GPL-3.0 (GitHub's API, `repos/golangci/golangci-lint/license`, 2026-10-21), so it would have to come in as a named exception (R12 spec). It is worth returning to with real code;
- to install the checks' tools into the image: `go run` at an exact version does without it;
- a list of licenses of its own: it is one for all repositories and comes from `go.yml` (R16 .github);
- the task in `module-kit`'s own session: the author chose to have the session of `.github` do it together with T15 `.github`.

— *Consequence:* Д2 stays: CodeQL does not read Go until the shared `codeql.yml` accepts it (T19 .github). The image with Go was not built here — Д4 in table 4 of RUN.md, and gopls is not in the image — Д5. R02 and R03 have amendments. — [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 1, 3.1 and 4; RUN.md, T10; README.md; `.devcontainer/`; `go.mod`; `cmd/module-kit/`; `justfile`; `.github/`.

— *Amendment 2026-10-21:* Д2 is closed: CodeQL reads Go with the shared `codeql.yml` of `.github` v0.1.3 (R06).

— *Amendment 2026-10-21:* the version of the CLI is written in by the recipe `release-files`, and the release is put out by the shared `release.yml` of `.github` (R07).

— *Amendment 2026-11-02:* Go 1.27.3 is now in the stage `toolchain` of `spec` itself, with the same version and the same sha256 checksums: with it `spec` checks its own Go module (R64 spec). So the container of `module-kit` no longer differs from the container of `spec` (CLAUDE.md, Environment): `.devcontainer/Dockerfile` is byte for byte what the recipe of `spec` builds, and the lines of this entry are not in it. With them `/home/vscode/go/bin` left `PATH` — `spec` does not have it either. The recipes here run through `go run` (R04), and a tool that a person installs with `go install` — gopls, for example, Д5 in table 4 of RUN.md — is called in the terminal by its full path, `~/go/bin/<name>`. Go is still raised by hand, but now by a move to a tag of `spec` where `spec` raised it (R08).

— *Amendment 2026-11-04:* `ci-test` also writes a coverage profile, `coverage/go.out`, and the shared `go.yml` v0.1.15 sends it to Codecov (R18). gopls is now in the image: v0.24.0 from the lock file `.devcontainer/gopls/`, which S8545 does not flag (R19). So the container of `module-kit` again differs from the container of `spec`, and Д5 is closed. — RUN.md, T18.

— *Second amendment 2026-11-04:* the module now has two dependencies — the Go modules `spec` and `spec/contracts` —, and Dependabot `gomod` does not raise them. `ci-lint` also checks their versions against the submodule `spec/`, and `ci-test` counts coverage for each package of the module, `-coverpkg=./...` (R20). — RUN.md, T19.

— *Amendment 2026-11-12:* Go in the stage `toolchain` is 1.27.4 instead of 1.27.3: `spec` raised it (the 2026-11-11 amendment to R64 spec), and it came here by the move to `spec` v0.16.3, together with `go 1.27.4` in the lock file of gopls (R54).

## The tag of `spec` v0.2.13 and CodeQL for Go

**R05. `module-kit` is on `spec` v0.2.13: the submodule `spec/` is at this tag, the links to `spec` pinned to a tag name it. The tag brings the started epic E2 of `spec` — the module format v0, which T01 and T02 waited for — and R35 spec on a task's review, and the change by R35 spec was made here even earlier by the session of `.github`.**

The row E2 left table 2, which [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 2, T01 and В1 referred to. The condition «начало эпика E2 `spec`» of T01 and T02 in table 1 of RUN.md is met. What E2 waits for from `module-kit`:
    - T34 `spec` — the module package and the index (Т-15, the term for the part with the digests — В8 `spec`) — waits for T01;

    - T36 `spec` — the issue and pull request templates (Д4 `spec`) — waits for the first code of `module-kit`, and the skeleton of the CLI is here since T10 (R04): whether to count it as the first code for T36, `spec` decides — В5 in table 3 of RUN.md, «в реестр spec». T37 `spec` — what checks the package's signature (Т-27) — waits for the first release of `module-kit`, and T38 `spec` tests the format v0 on the first content of `modules` by the check of `module-kit` in CI;
  - v0.2.13 — R35 spec: a task's review is one round of `/code-review high`, and the executor may hand in a small change without a review; rules 6 and 8 of the template's RUN.md and its AGENTS.md say the same. Here this change was made on 2026-10-21 by the session of `.github` on the author's instruction, pull request #9, commit cacee12 — a one-time departure from R21 spec, as R35 spec records. After it the rules of RUN.md up to «Сделанных эпиков» match the template of v0.2.13 line for line, but for the substituted values, and AGENTS.md matches it byte for byte;

В1 and В5 are closed: `spec` answered В1 — a template's key is computed in advance, over all instances (R60 spec; Д42 in table 4 of RUN.md of `spec`) —, and В5 by the 2026-10-22 amendment to R22 spec.

**R06. CodeQL reads Go: `.github/workflows/codeql.yml` calls the shared `codeql.yml` of `.github` v0.1.3 — commit 26f6199 — with the input `languages: go`. This is the only link to `.github` that was raised by hand, ahead of Dependabot: it needs a new input. The other links are still raised by Dependabot (T08). The author puts the required check "CodeQL / analyze (go)" in the ruleset `main`.**
- **How it reads Go** (`codeql.yml` of `.github` v0.1.3, commit 26f6199, and the 2026-10-21 amendment to R09 .github, read 2026-10-21):
  - CodeQL reads Go by a build: the analysis runs with `build-mode: autobuild`, and the step analyze builds the database itself;
  - the Go for the build is the one that `module-kit` has in the stage `toolchain` of `.devcontainer/Dockerfile`: the job builds this stage, copies `/usr/local/go` onto the runner ahead of the runner's own Go and sets `GOTOOLCHAIN=local`. So the analysis runs with Go 1.27.3, from the archive that the Dockerfile checked against its checksum (R04) — with the same Go as the checks "Go" (R16 .github). When Go is raised in the Dockerfile, the analysis will run with the new one;
  - the queries are `security-extended`, the results are under the category `/language:go`, and the findings hold the merge by the ruleset's rule for CodeQL — errors and vulnerabilities of high and above (R30 spec; R02). `actions` is analysed as before, always;
  - the autobuild of Go first runs `make`, `ninja`, `./build` and `./build.sh` in turn, until one of the commands and the `go list ./...` that follows it pass, and only then installs the dependencies by `go.mod` and extracts the code, as `go build ./...`; the tests, the files `_test.go`, are not analysed by default ([architecture.md](architecture.md), section 4). `module-kit` has neither a Makefile, nor `build.ninja`, nor `build` and `build.sh` in its root: the build is run by the `justfile`. If such a file appears, the analysis of Go will need the mode `manual` (the amendment to R09 .github).

— *Why:* Д2 in table 4 of RUN.md waited for the shared `codeql.yml` to accept Go: since T10 Go code came to `main` without CodeQL's analysis, and the ruleset's rule for CodeQL's findings had nothing to hold. `.github` v0.1.3 accepted it — T19 .github, the amendment to R09 .github. The release is immutable; `gh release verify v0.1.3 -R altnovatrail/.github` — "Release v0.1.3 verified!", and the tag is on commit 26f6199 (`git ls-remote`, 2026-10-21). The input `languages: go` and the link to v0.1.3 were the author's instruction of 2026-10-21.

— *Rejected:*
- to wait until Dependabot raises the link: it will not add the input, and without `go` Go would go on without analysis. The three-day pause would hold the raise itself too (T08);
- to raise the other links by hand too — `secrets.yml`, `dco.yml`, `documents.yml`, `go.yml` and `scorecard.yml`: they need no new input, and their raise is what T08 tests Dependabot on;
- to name `go` and leave the link at v0.1.0: that version accepts, beyond `actions`, only `javascript-typescript`, and the check "CodeQL / languages" would stop with a reason, and the required "CodeQL / analyze (actions)" would not come and would hold up any merge (Д9 in table 4 of RUN.md of `spec`);
- CodeQL's default setup in the repository's settings: with it GitHub turns off the advanced setup — the shared workflow —, and the analysis of Go would not run with the Go from `toolchain` (the comment of `codeql.yml` of `.github` v0.1.3).

— *Consequence:* Д2 is closed. "CodeQL / analyze (go)" will become required when the author puts it in the ruleset `main` 25488826 (rule 4 of RUN.md; R30 spec); until then the ruleset's rule for CodeQL holds the findings of Go, but not the check itself. The links to `.github` now stand at three releases: `codeql.yml` at v0.1.3, `go.yml` at v0.1.2, the rest at v0.1.0; T08 does not change this and raises the others when Dependabot proposes them. Д9 in table 4 of RUN.md of `spec` at v0.2.13 still says that the shared `codeql.yml` does not accept Go — this is a debt of `spec`, not a question to it. R02 and R04 have amendments. — `.github/workflows/codeql.yml`; RUN.md, T12, table 4; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 1 and 3.1.

## Release

**R07. The release of `module-kit` is put out by the shared `release.yml` of `.github` v0.1.4 — commit 11435db —, and the files for it are built by a recipe of its own, `release-files`: the CLI as one binary per platform — linux, darwin and windows, each on amd64 and arm64 —, two licenses and `SHA256SUMS`. The number is the next patch of the line `0.1`, and a release comes out only if what it is built from has changed.**
- **The calling `.github/workflows/release.yml`** — word for word the text of architecture 3.3 .github, with the link to v0.1.4 (R18 .github):
  - it runs on `workflow_run` after CI on `main` and by the button; the shared job itself checks that CI on this commit is green and that it is a push from this repository;
  - `RELEASE_LINE: "0.1"`: by it the release recipes of `spec` from the submodule count the number, and the first release is v0.1.0;
  - `paths` — everything but `docs/`, the files `*.md`, `.github/` and the submodule `spec`. A release is given by the code and its tests, `go.mod`, `justfile`, `LICENSE`, and also by the whole `.devcontainer/`, `.claude/`, `.gitignore` and `.sonarcloud.properties`: the list is the exclusions, not a list of what the binary is built from. So a release is also given by changes that do not change the binary — the version of Claude Code or gh in the stage `devcontainer`, a test — and by a move to a new tag of `spec` if the recipe of `spec` changes the Dockerfile (the amendment to R03). It is not given by a change of the documents or of CI — the change of `RELEASE_LINE` included — or by the tag of `spec` itself: a new line will start with the next change that gives a release. The submodule `spec` does not get into the build at all: `git archive` does not take it, and the code will take the schemas and test vectors of `spec` from the releases of `spec`, not from the submodule (R21 spec) — as files or as a module in `go.mod`, that is, inside `paths`. The template of `paths` is shared by all repositories — В6 in table 3 of RUN.md, «в реестр spec». The first release always comes out;
  - the job has no `concurrency` of its own: the shared job itself stands in the queue `release`.
- **The recipe `release-files <version>`** — in the `justfile`, by the conditions of the shared workflow: the stage `toolchain` of the commit being released, its tree without history and submodules, without git, under the runner's uid, with `HOME=/tmp`, without tokens:
  - it takes the version as an argument and accepts only the form `v<major>.<minor>.<patch>`, with a suffix after a hyphen or without; otherwise it stops;
  - it deletes `dist/`, creates it anew and writes only regular files into it, with names by `^[A-Za-z0-9_+][A-Za-z0-9._+-]*$`, without `.sigstore.json`;
  - the CLI — `go build` for six platforms, `CGO_ENABLED=0`, `-trimpath`, `-buildvcs=false` and `-ldflags "-s -w -X main.version=<version>"`: `module-kit --version` answers with the release version. The name is `module-kit_<version>_<os>_<arch>`, for windows with `.exe`. The caller's Go environment does not get into the build: the recipe sets `GOENV=off`, empty `GOFLAGS` and `GOEXPERIMENT`, and unsets `GOAMD64`, `GOARM64`, `GOOS` and `GOARCH`;
  - a CLI built without `-X` — for example by `go install github.com/altnovatrail/module-kit/cmd/module-kit@v0.1.0` — takes the version from the build information that Go puts into the binary, `runtime/debug.ReadBuildInfo`, and where there is none, answers `dev`;
  - `LICENSE` — the Apache-2.0 of `module-kit`, and `LICENSE-go` — Go's license from `$(go env GOROOT)` of the same stage ([architecture.md](architecture.md), section 4);
  - `SHA256SUMS` — the checksums of all the files above, in the format of `sha256sum`, so that `sha256sum -c SHA256SUMS` checks the whole set at once;
  - `dist/` — in `.gitignore`.
- **What the shared workflow does next** (R18 .github): it signs each file with cosign 3.1.4 without keys, puts its `.sigstore.json` beside it, checks each bundle before publishing, publishes the release as Latest and checks it with `gh release verify` and `gh release verify-asset`. Immutable releases are turned on for `module-kit`, and the tags `v*` are held by the ruleset "v-tags" 25559103 — `update`, `deletion` and `non_fast_forward`, without bypass. They were set by the executor of `.github` with the author's consent (GitHub's API, 2026-10-21).

— *Why:* the author's instruction of 2026-10-21 (RUN.md, T13): the first release of `module-kit` goes through the shared `release.yml`, which T16 .github put out in v0.1.4. The signature of this release is the signature of the shared workflow, and by it T37 `spec` decides Т-27. What to build, the author left to the executor:
- six platforms — because the CLI is for module authors (architecture 3.7 spec), and they work on macOS and Windows too, arm64 included. How CI of `modules` and of `canvas` takes the CLI — as a release binary with a check of its signature, or by `go install` at a tag — they decide together with `spec`: В7 in table 3 of RUN.md, «в реестр spec»;
- a binary without an archive: each file is signed and checked on its own, and whoever takes the CLI in CI downloads and checks one file. An archive is one more layer, which has to be unpacked, and inside it the bundle would sign the archive, not the binary;
- `CGO_ENABLED=0` — a static binary without the host's libc; `-trimpath` and `-buildvcs=false` — without build paths and without the VCS stamp, which the release tree does not have, so the build is reproducible: from the tree of `git archive` and from a working copy it gave the same checksums (RUN.md, T13, «Сделано»);
- two licenses in the release — because the binary is distributed without its sources: Apache-2.0 requires giving a copy of it, and Go's license requires reproducing its notice on redistribution in binary form ([architecture.md](architecture.md), section 4). `module-kit` has no third-party modules, and when they appear, their licenses are checked by `ci-licenses` (R04).

— *Rejected:*
- archives `.tar.gz` and `.zip` with the binary and the licenses — the reason is above; the licenses lie beside it anyway, and `SHA256SUMS` checks them;
- only linux amd64 — authors on macOS and Windows would build the CLI themselves, from the sources;
- goreleaser — one more tool with a configuration of its own, while the shared workflow signs and publishes: only `go build` is left to the recipe;
- cgo — the code does not need it, and with it the binary would depend on libc and each platform would need a C compiler of its own;
- the VCS stamp of `go build` by default — the release tree has no git, and a build from a working copy would differ from the release's build;
- a release without the licenses — it would breach a condition of Go's license;
- a `concurrency` of its own for the caller's job — the shared job and the caller's job would wait for each other (architecture 3.3 .github).
- a `paths` of its own — a list of what the binary is built from, instead of the exclusions from architecture 3.3 .github: the author said to take the text word for word, and the template is shared by all repositories — В6 in table 3 of RUN.md, «в реестр spec».

— *Consequence:* after the merge of T13 the first release comes out by itself — v0.1.0 —, and the CLI answers with its version also when it is installed by `go install` at a tag; its check and the certificate's extensions for T37 `spec` are in T13, «Итог после мержа». The binaries other than linux amd64 were not run here — Д6 in table 4 of RUN.md. The link to `.github` in `release.yml` is raised by Dependabot, as the others are (T08). R04 has an amendment. — `.github/workflows/release.yml`; `justfile`; `.gitignore`; RUN.md, T13, table 4; [architecture.md](architecture.md), the status, sections 1, 3.1 and 4.

## The tag of `spec` v0.6.3

**R08. `module-kit` is on `spec` v0.6.3, on the line 0.6, from which the repositories follow the cross-repository plan of `spec` (R76 spec): the submodule `spec/` is at this tag, the links to `spec` pinned to a tag name it, and `.devcontainer/` is byte for byte what the recipe of `spec` v0.6.3 builds, and Go 1.27.3 in it is the Go of `spec` itself. The tag brings the module format 0.2 and closes Т-15 and Т-18 without T01 and T02, and with them the rows В1, В3, В5, В6 and В7 of table 3 of RUN.md. T01–T05 are narrowed to what the format left to `module-kit`.**
- **What the tag brings `module-kit`** (`git diff v0.2.13 v0.6.3` in `spec/`, read 2026-11-02; the rows of table 4 of RUN.md of `spec` where `module-kit` is named, and COORDINATION.md of `spec`):
  - the cross-repository plan — R76 spec. The work of the repositories is led by COORDINATION.md of `spec`, and its goal is the first app: the author solves tasks in the browser themselves. `module-kit` has three rows there: 3.1 — this move, 3.2 — the rest of E1 together with T08, 3.3 — the code epic, the whole format 0.2. The first release of `module-kit` with code passes all the test vectors of the format, the Starlark predicates, templates and solvers too (Д53 in table 4 of RUN.md of `spec`). The first batch of tasks of `modules` goes without templates and solvers (repositories 6 spec);
  - the module format 0.2 — in the folder `format/` of `spec`, in English (R42 spec):
    - the format version is MAJOR.MINOR, and the window of versions, while the format is v0, is only the current one: `module-kit` rejects a module of 0.1 (R44 spec; Д59 there too);
    - JSON Schema 2020-12 over YAML 1.2.2 with constraints, format rules with IDs, the open view by the marks `x-open` (R45 spec; Т-7 closed);
    - the module layout and IDs (R46 spec), the skill graph with tiers and retired skills (R49 spec), the shared taxonomy (R50 spec; Т-14 closed), tasks (R51 spec), check kinds (R56 spec), traps, techniques and the leak check (R57 spec), lessons (R58 spec), templates (R60 spec; О-12 decided), solvers (R61 spec) and the module index (R63 spec).

    The card 3.7 spec names what of this `module-kit` executes, and by parts — Д20–Д22, Д30–Д32, Д37–Д45 and Д57–Д59 in table 4 of RUN.md of `spec`. This is the code epic, row 3.3;
  - Т-15 is closed by R62 spec, without T01. The package is the canonical view, the device part and the inventory; the canonical text is by RFC 8785; the package digest is the digest of the inventory. The term for the part that names the digests of both parts is "inventory" (concepts 15 spec). The card left `module-kit` one thing — what to write the canonical text with: Go's `encoding/json` escapes what the format text writes as it is (architecture 3.7 spec; Д44 in table 4 of RUN.md of `spec`);
  - Т-18 is closed by R59 spec, without T02. The generator is PCG from Go's `math/rand/v2`; the stream of a place of randomness is derived as in standalone's simulator — FNV-1a 64 of the seed and the names of the place. The card left one thing — how CI of `module-kit` passes the generator vectors: with Go's standard library or with its own code (Д40 there too);
  - Starlark — the format decided the steps:
    - a check predicate — 1,000,000 steps, and a limit reached or an error gives "unchecked" (R56 spec);
    - a template solver runs only at the module check — 100,000,000 steps for all the instances of a template, in the dialect of go.starlark.net with the options of standalone, without `load` and recursion (R61 spec);
    - the key of a template is computed in advance, for all the instances, and lies in the package (R60 spec; R62 spec). So `spec` answered В1 too: «Его В1 — где считать ключ шаблона — отвечен» (Д42 there too).

    The card left the clock and concurrent runs to T03 `module-kit`;
  - the version of a module:
    - the mapping table is `retired_skills` of the skill graph, and the author declares a change of a skill's meaning by a new ID (R49 spec);
    - a module's transition to a new format version is a new version of the module (R44 spec).

    T04 decides which version part this transition and an edit of `teaches` and `dependencies` (R50 spec) raise and by what rules two versions are checked against each other. The rules of `diff` come into the format text by a row «в реестр spec» (R49 spec; Д30 and Д31 there too);

The schemas, examples and vectors come from `spec.Format`, the types from `types/formatv0` and `types/formatv0/open`, and the module `spec` is excluded from Dependabot `gomod` (R64 spec, R72 spec). This enters the code together with the first code that reads the format (Д46 there too);

The recipe `editDevcontainer` adds the suffix `spec` to the decisions that the Dockerfile refers to. R40 and R79 spec do not concern the kind `go`;

R36–R39, R43, R47, R52–R55, R65–R71, R73–R75 and R78–R80 spec are about the interface, AI, privacy and other components. They do not change the documents of `module-kit`, except for what is named above.

Then Release on `main` would stop after the merge of #13: since v0.1.6 the shared release requires the submodule `spec` at v0.2.17 or newer (architecture 3.3 .github). And the merge of a pull request of Dependabot is what T08 checks. Nobody takes the skeleton of the CLI, and the release v0.1.1, invalid by R77 spec, breaks nothing, like v0.1.0;
