- **what was done in the code:**
  - **the window of versions** — `module.FormatVersion` is `0.4`, and `ReadsFormat` accepts only it ([format text](../spec/format/README.md), section 2). The copy of the schemas `internal/schema/schemas/` is v0.12.0, with two new ones: `page.schema.json` and `page-vector.schema.json` (R23);
  - **`yaml.printable`** — a character outside the printable set of YAML 1.2.2, wherever it stands, in quotes too, the reader rejected before as well, but without an ID, as "not YAML" (R45 spec); now the refusal names the rule ([format text](../spec/format/README.md), section 4);
  - **template expressions** begin with a token and are nested no deeper than 64 levels — a parenthesis, a unary `-` and `not`. This is now a rule of the subset, in `typeOf`: the dialect parses every expression of the subset, and the check against go.starlark.net stayed as a second line: an expression of the subset that the dialect still does not parse is a breach, not a panic of `compiled` ([format text](../spec/format/README.md), section 23; В28 — R102 spec; R32);
  - **the kind `time`** — `normal.Time` and `normal.TimeNotation` read a time by the steps 1–5 of a number, `H:MM` or `HH:MM` with minutes of two digits, or of three that begin with no zero, and `normal.Clock` compares the hours and the minutes as numbers, without a sum of minutes: `5:60` is not `6:00`. The verdict `timeVerdict`, the traps, `NormalForm` and the join of two readings — by the same. The leak check finds a key of a time where a time of the text has the same hours and minutes, and a time of a text is a run of digits, `:` and digits in the notation of a time with no character of a word at its edges ([format text](../spec/format/README.md), sections 15 and 20; R111 spec);
  - **`notation: word`** of a choice written by hand — `normal.ChoiceOf` (in `internal/normal/choice.go`) also drops `.`, `!`, `?` and spaces at the end, and one word of letters that is not among the options is `not_yet`, and `normal.Word` recognizes it: a letter of the category L, then letters and marks M. `task.choice-key` requires of the key one word, its end as written, and `task.choice-options` no option that normalization would leave empty ([format text](../spec/format/README.md), section 15);
  - **the picture paths of a template** hold placeholders: an instance substitutes the numbers into all the strings, the path too, `task.known-pictures` now checks the path of each instance, and in a task that is not a template a brace in a path is a breach of `task.placeholders`, as is a placeholder of a solver's result in any path, in the path of a picture of the solution too: `takesResults` lets a result only into the text of the solution. The picture path of an instance is among the texts of `task.no-leaks`, the path of a task without a template is not ([format text](../spec/format/README.md), sections 20 and 23);
  - **the leak check** also searches the leading question `leading_question` — in the file of the task —, the title and the description of each solving step — a text of the whole module, for each task —, the texts of the numbered list of a lesson, and in a worked example on a workbook the leak phrases in the texts of its lines, but not the keys, and the line of the example that solves a line of the task: the same pieces in the same order, the texts in the normal form, the value of each box equal to the key, as the check of the box compares it. The normal form of the search is `normal.Search`: `leak` searches by it, the module check compares the texts of lines by it too, and `leak` gained no exported name ([format text](../spec/format/README.md), section 20). The check now reads the solving steps as a part of their own, `parts.steps`, and warns about a number in them, as about a number in a text of the whole module (R50);
  - **the workbook page** — the package `page`: `Lay` lays out the item lines by the numbers and the width table of `page.yaml` ([format text](../spec/format/README.md), section 14) and gives the places of the number, the texts and the boxes, or `*NoRoom` with the item whose row has no room; `TextWidth` and `Fits` give the width of a text and whether a value fits in a box. A copy of `page.yaml` is embedded in the package and held by a test byte for byte against `spec.Format`, as the copy of the schemas is: the package does not import `spec`, and a consumer of the library carries one file, not all the files of the format. On it stand `task.workbook-page`, on a task without a template and on each instance, and `lesson.workbook-page` with `lesson.example-fills` — in `module/workbook.go`. The message about a lack of room is one: with the numbers of `page.yaml` the rows end at about the twentieth item, long before `most_items`, 99;
  - **version comparison** — for a choice the meaning is held by `notation` too — `options` without it —, and by the options in the normal form of that notation; for a time, by the key and the traps by hours and minutes. The leading question and the description of a step are a patch by the general rule "anything else" ([format text](../spec/format/README.md), section 27);
  - **the index build** already refused a previous index of a newer format version and one that the schema of its own version does not accept (`ErrPrevious`, R35) — what 0.4 wrote into the [format text](../spec/format/README.md), section 26; no edit was needed;
  - **the vector test** knows the kind `pages/` — `pages_test.go` —, the verdict kind `time`, `page.yaml` as a file that is no case, and `page-vector.schema.json` of the page vectors. The vectors of `versions/` of the tag — packages of previous format versions by places — the code passed without edits. `TestDigestsRefuse` (R51) — without changes;
  - **tests** — new ones: `normal` — the time and the notation `word`; `leak` — the key of a time; `verdict` — a time without a key and with a key not in notation; `module` — `solves`, the search for phrases in the lines of an example, an empty option of a choice `word`, a lack of room and a value too wide; `moduleversion` — the meaning of the notation and of the time; `page` — 99.7% of the lines, a fuzz test, a check against the layout of `spec` `tools/format-check/page.mjs` on 20,000 random tasks without divergences, by a one-time probe. The tests that called 0.3 the current version are shifted by one version: the current one is 0.4, the previous 0.3, the future 0.5;
- **the release** — `RELEASE_LINE` is `0.3`, and the merge will give v0.3.0. The line 0.2, v0.2.0–v0.2.7, reads format 0.3, and the line 0.3 reads 0.4; `page/page.yaml` is in the list `paths`, since the binary embeds it (R18);
- **the container** — the Dockerfile from the template's generator `tools/new-repo/` for the kind `go` at v0.12.0, into a temporary folder, as in R51: only the comment of the gopls step changed, and it says itself that the copy is held only by the repository's own check — in `module-kit` this is the check of `ci-lint`;
- **checked:** `gh release verify v0.12.0 -R altnovatrail/spec` — "Release v0.12.0 verified!". `ci-lint`, `ci-test` — all the cases of `spec.Format` and `contracts.Vectors` of the tag without skips —, `ci-vuln`, `ci-licenses` with the list of licenses from `go.yml` of `.github` and `just -f spec/justfile docs-check-repo` are green.

— *Why:* the author's instruction of 2026-11-09 — to move to the latest version of `spec`, without an epic and a task of its own, and to bring it to the merge. Row 3.8 of COORDINATION.md of `spec` names exactly this: format 0.4 in the code, the window on 0.4 and a release of its own —, and `modules` moves to 0.4 only after a release of `module-kit` with it (row 3.9). A new line: in versions 0.y of semver an incompatible change is carried by the minor number, and a release that rejects every module of 0.3 is an incompatible change for `modules`, `canvas`, `api` and the eval runner of `tutor`.

— *Rejected:*
- an epic of tasks, as E2 for format 0.3: the author instructed the move without an epic and a task. The vector test without skips (R42) holds anyway that the whole format is in the code, and there are no skips to merge in parts;
- staying on the line 0.2: v0.2.8 would reject the modules of 0.3 that v0.2.7 reads — an incompatible change in a patch;
- the page layout in `module`: the package `page` is needed by others too — `api`, the check of instances —, and holds its own copy of `page.yaml` and its own vectors, as `picture` holds its decoder (R39);
- taking `page.yaml` from `spec.Format` at run time: the root of the module `spec` embeds all the files of the format, and `api` would carry them (R23);
- a window on 0.3 and 0.4 at once: while the format is v0, the platform reads only the current version ([format text](../spec/format/README.md), section 2).

— *Consequence:* the merge will give the release v0.3.0, the first of the line 0.3, — row 3.9 of COORDINATION.md of `spec` waits for it: `modules` moves the modules to 0.4, and CI of `modules` and `canvas` to the binary v0.3.0, having checked it with `release-verify`; `api` and the eval runner of `tutor` take the Go module at the tag v0.3.0. Table 4 of RUN.md: Д18 is closed. — `spec/`; `go.mod`, `go.sum`; `internal/schema/schemas/`; `internal/yaml/`; `internal/normal/`; `verdict/`; `observation/`; `leak/`; `template/`; `module/`; `moduleversion/`; `page/`; `internal/vectors/`; the tests of `moduleindex/`, `modulepackage/` and `cmd/module-kit/`; `.devcontainer/Dockerfile`; `.github/workflows/release.yml`; README.md; CLAUDE.md; RUN.md, the start and table 4; [architecture.md](architecture.md), the status, sections 1 and 3.1.

## The token savings of sessions

**R55. `module-kit` takes the order of work and the structure of the documents of R143 spec and R145 spec by two tasks. T43 — `.claude/settings.json` by the template of the kind `go`, the rules of parts and a thin CLAUDE.md, the rules of RUN.md by the template and `DEBT.md`, the sources and the measurements in files of their own, the budgets of size. T44 — the translation of `docs/` into English. Beyond the template — one case of rule 9 of RUN.md: when there is no current epic, the body of a done task outside an epic goes to the archive of the last done epic. Until T44, rule 10 of RUN.md and Documents in CLAUDE.md also differ from the template: `docs/` are still in Russian.**
- **What was taken** (T43; row 7.1 of COORDINATION.md spec; T123 spec):
  - `.claude/settings.json` — byte for byte what `tools/new-repo/` of `spec` v0.16.3 builds for the kind `go`. From the template's file — `claudeMdExcludes` with the line of Д215 spec, the compaction at 400 thousand, the claude.ai connectors turned off and the hook `UserPromptSubmit` from `spec/tools/session/`; from the kind `go` — the marketplace `novatrail` in `./spec/claude-plugins` and `gopls-lsp@novatrail` (R143 spec and its 2026-11-12 amendment). The plugin runs gopls from `PATH` — the one that the stage `devcontainer` builds from the lock file `.devcontainer/gopls/` (R19, R51). The file was built by the function `addClaudeSettings` from `spec/tools/new-repo/claude.mjs` in a temporary folder, and `checkClaudePlugins` from the same place found the marketplace and the plugin;
  - the rules of parts: `.claude/rules/go.md` — the Go code, `go.mod`, `go.sum` and the embedded copies of the format's files, with the checks `ci-lint`, `ci-test`, `ci-vuln` and `ci-licenses`, — and `.claude/rules/ci.md` — CI, the release and the devcontainer. Before, the checks of the parts were only in [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.1, and in the comments of the justfile, and a rule gives them to a session as soon as the session opens a file of the part. CLAUDE.md names both rules, and its Environment also the documents check with budgets and `.claude/settings.json`;
  - RUN.md — the rules «Как пользоваться» and «Как устроен план: эпики» of the template v0.16.3. This is one task — one session; the body of a done task goes to the archive at once; table 4 is in [DEBT.md](../DEBT.md); when an epic is archived, a measurement of the tokens spent (R145 spec). There were no bodies of done tasks in RUN.md: E1 and E2 are already in the archive;
  - "Sources" — the 47 items of [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 4. 44 of them became records of [sources.md](sources.md), under six topics, and the 3 measurements — of the sandbox, of the YAML readers and of the size of `spec.Format` — became [measurements.md](measurements.md), section 1. The items moved word for word, and each record begins with the decision or the section it was read for; the results of the measurement of the sandbox, for which the item referred to R11, are copied into measurements 1.1. The claims that cited architecture 4 and the external facts of architecture 3.1 and 3.6 cite the records, and the numbers of the measurement in architecture 3.4 were replaced by a reference to measurements 1.1. [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 4, became a pointer: the log and the archives refer to it, and they are not rewritten (the 2026-11-12 amendment to R143 spec);
  - [docs-budgets.json](../docs-budgets.json) — the limit of the section architecture 3.6, 84 KB of 1,024 bytes, over the block `repository`: in Russian the section takes 76,445 bytes, ≈ 74.7 KB, and the limit is the size plus 10–20%, as in measurements 1.9 spec. T44 will lower or remove the limit, by the size of the translation (R145 spec).
- **The split.** The translation is ≈ 835 KB, more than one check (rule 11 of RUN.md). `translation-check <ref>` checks the translation against the commit before it, so the restructuring goes as a task and a commit of its own, and the whole of `docs/` is translated by one task, T44, as T138 spec translated 924 KB by one task.
- **Without a current epic.** Rule 9 of the template sends the body of a task outside an epic to the archive of the current epic, and `module-kit` has none: E2 is done, and the next one is not chosen. The body goes to the archive of the last done epic, to the section «Задачи вне эпика, сделанные после него» — now this is [plan/E2.md](plan/E2.md). The gist sentence stands at the start of «Задачи вне эпика» of RUN.md and leaves it together with the next epic. So says rule 9 of RUN.md, and `spec` gets the question as row В35 of RUN.md.

— *Why:* T123 spec gives each repository one task outside an epic, and a session whose task is more than one check proposes to split it. On 2026-11-12 the author chose two tasks and the archive of E2. Without the case "no epic" the body of T43 would stay in RUN.md, against R143 spec, and an archive file of its own would have to be remembered by the search for a task by its heading.

— *Rejected:*
- three tasks — architecture and sources apart from the log — and four, with the log in halves: more sessions and checks, and with four, `translation-check` of the log goes red between the tasks on the untranslated half;
- the restructuring and the translation by one task, with the check against `git stash create`, as in T136 spec: in the review, the diff of the translation would hide the edit of the restructuring;
- an epic E3 of T43 and T44: T123 spec sets a task outside an epic;
- the bodies wait in RUN.md for the next epic: against "the body of a done task goes to the epic's archive at once" of R143 spec. An archive file of its own for tasks outside an epic was rejected by the 2026-11-12 amendment to R143 spec;
- `sources.md` in English at once: that would be a translation without a check, since `translation-check` has no original. As in `spec`, the records move in Russian, and T44 translates them (T133 spec, T137 spec);
- one rule `go.md`, without `ci.md`: the checks of CI and of the release would stay scattered over architecture 3.1 and the justfile.
